00166: def mesh_object(name,v,f,color,parent_name):
00167:     me=bpy.data.meshes.new(name)
00168:     me.from_pydata(v,[],f)
00169:     me.update()
00170:     ob=bpy.data.objects.new(name,me)
00171:     bpy.context.collection.objects.link(ob)
00172:     root=bpy.data.objects.get(parent_name)
00173:     if root:
00174:         ob.parent=root
00175:         ob.matrix_parent_inverse=root.matrix_world.inverted()
00176:     mat=bpy.data.materials.new(name+'_material')
00177:     mat.diffuse_color=(*color,1)
00178:     mat.use_nodes=True
00179:     bs=mat.node_tree.nodes['Principled BSDF']
00180:     bs.inputs['Base Color'].default_value=(*color,1)
00181:     bs.inputs['Roughness'].default_value=.92
00182:     ob.data.materials.append(mat)
00183:     return ob

00177: def descendants(obj):
00178:     yield obj
00179:     for child in obj.children:
00180:         yield from descendants(child)

00182: def material_for_group(group_name, factory):
00183:     marker = bpy.data.objects.get(group_name)
00184:     if marker:
00185:         for obj in descendants(marker):
00186:             if obj.type == 'MESH':
00187:                 obj.data.materials.clear()
00188:                 obj.data.materials.append(factory())

00190: def wood_material():
00191:     m = bpy.data.materials.new('Warm oak vertical grain')
00192:     m.use_nodes = True
00193:     n, l = m.node_tree.nodes, m.node_tree.links
00194:     bs = n.get('Principled BSDF')
00195:     bs.inputs['Roughness'].default_value = .62
00196:     tex = n.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise')
00197:     tex.inputs['Scale'].default_value = 4
00198:     tex.inputs['Detail'].default_value = 3.5
00199:     coord = n.new('ShaderNodeTexCoord')
00200:     mapping = n.new('ShaderNodeVectorMath')
00201:     mapping.operation = 'MULTIPLY'
00202:     mapping.inputs[1].default_value = (7, 7, .20)
00203:     l.new(coord.outputs['Generated'], mapping.inputs[0])
00204:     l.new(mapping.outputs[0], tex.inputs['Vector'])
00205:     ramp = n.new('ShaderNodeValToRGB')
00206:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = .20
00207:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (.16, .075, .028, 1)
00208:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = .82
00209:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (.51, .33, .18, 1)
00210:     l.new(tex.outputs['Fac'], ramp.inputs[0])
00211:     l.new(ramp.outputs[0], bs.inputs['Base Color'])
00212:     bump = n.new('ShaderNodeBump')
00213:     bump.inputs['Strength'].default_value = .19
00214:     bump.inputs['Distance'].default_value = .016
00215:     l.new(tex.outputs['Fac'], bump.inputs['Height'])

00181: # Procedural wood grain and lightly mottled pale stone, applied through groups.
00182: def descendants(obj):
00183:     yield obj
00184:     for child in obj.children:
00185:         yield from descendants(child)
00186: 
00187: def material_for_group(group_name, factory):
00188:     marker = bpy.data.objects.get(group_name)
00189:     if marker:
00190:         for obj in descendants(marker):
00191:             if obj.type == 'MESH':
00192:                 mat = factory()
00193:                 obj.data.materials.clear()
00194:                 obj.data.materials.append(mat)

00196: def wood_material():
00197:     m = bpy.data.materials.new('Warm oak vertical grain')
00198:     m.use_nodes = True
00199:     n, l = m.node_tree.nodes, m.node_tree.links
00200:     bs = n.get('Principled BSDF')
00201:     bs.inputs['Roughness'].default_value = .62
00202:     tex = n.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise')
00203:     tex.inputs['Scale'].default_value = 4.0
00204:     tex.inputs['Detail'].default_value = 3.5
00205:     coord = n.new('ShaderNodeTexCoord')
00206:     mapping = n.new('ShaderNodeVectorMath')
00207:     mapping.operation = 'MULTIPLY'
00208:     mapping.inputs[1].default_value = (7,7,.20)
00209:     l.new(coord.outputs['Generated'], mapping.inputs[0])
00210:     l.new(mapping.outputs[0], tex.inputs['Vector'])
00211:     ramp = n.new('ShaderNodeValToRGB')
00212:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = .20
00213:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (.16,.075,.028,1)
00214:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = .82
00215:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (.51,.33,.18,1)
00216:     l.new(tex.outputs['Fac'], ramp.inputs[0])
00217:     l.new(ramp.outputs[0], bs.inputs['Base Color'])
00218:     bump = n.new('ShaderNodeBump')
00219:     bump.inputs['Strength'].default_value = .19
00220:     bump.inputs['Distance'].default_value = .016
00221:     l.new(tex.outputs['Fac'], bump.inputs['Height'])
00222:     l.new(bump.outputs[0], bs.inputs['Normal'])
00223:     return m
00224:

00121:     yield obj

00125: def material_for_group(group_name,factory):
00126:     marker=bpy.data.objects.get(group_name)

00128:         for obj in descendants(marker):
00129:             if obj.type=='MESH':

00135:     m.use_nodes=True
00136:     n,l=m.node_tree.nodes,m.node_tree.links

00138:     bs.inputs['Roughness'].default_value=.62
00139:     tex=n.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise')

00177: def descendants(obj):
00178:     yield obj
00179:     for child in obj.children:
00180:         yield from descendants(child)
00181: 
00182: def material_for_group(group_name, factory):
00183:     marker = bpy.data.objects.get(group_name)
00184:     if marker:
00185:         for obj in descendants(marker):
00186:             if obj.type == 'MESH':
00187:                 obj.data.materials.clear()
00188:                 obj.data.materials.append(factory())

00203:     l.new(coord.outputs['Generated'], mapping.inputs[0])

00006: # feedback shows too much room, smaller machinery, and an exposed wall top.
00007: # Estimated reference landmarks: main wheel (340,95), small wheel (182,108),
00008: # anvil (220,149), foreground machine (402,206), floor marker (66,273).
00009: # Main correction family: camera translation and aim. Moving closer increases
00010: # foreground scale and parallax; the more oblique view steepens bench edges.
00011: # Camera remains an uncertain estimate; geometry dimensions are preserved.

00013: # vice -> bench vice; can -> floor container. All movable parts stay grouped.

00016: wood=(.25,.20,.13)
00017: topwood=(.37,.31,.22)

00023: def B(a,k,s,p,c):
00024:     return a.include(k,box(size=s,center=p,color=c))
00025: def R(a,k,p,q,r,c):
00026:     return a.include(k,rod(radius=r,start=p,end=q,color=c))
00027: def T(a,k,pts,r,c):
00028:     return a.include(k,sweep_tube(points=pts,radius=r,color=c))
00029: def ring(a,k,p,r,t,c,plane="xz"):
00030:     pts=[]
00031:     for j in range(25):
00032:         v=j*math.tau/24
00033:         if plane=="xz":
00034:             pts.append((p[0]+r*math.cos(v),p[1],p[2]+r*math.sin(v)))
00035:         elif plane=="xy":
00036:             pts.append((p[0]+r*math.cos(v),p[1]+r*math.sin(v),p[2]))
00037:         else:
00038:             pts.append((p[0],p[1]+r*math.cos(v),p[2]+r*math.sin(v)))
00039:     return T(a,k,pts,t,c)

00070: def flywheel(x,y,z,r):
00071:     a=Asset("cast iron flywheel")
00072:     ring(a,"rim",(x,y,z),r,.032,iron)
00073:     R(a,"hub",(x,y-.09,z),(x,y+.15,z),.065,steel)

00037:         else: p=(c[0],c[1]+r*math.cos(t),c[2]+r*math.sin(t))
00038:         pts.append(p)
00039:     return tube(a,k,pts,thick,color)
